Across all four classes, the reviewed studies consistently demonstrate improved predictive performance over conventional baselines across a broad range of assets, although the literature is heavily skewed toward lithium-ion batteries and bearings, and dominated by problem-specific solutions. Overall, the review indicates that physics-informed approaches already provide tangible benefits, whereas claims of improvements concerning some of the aforementioned limitations lack sufficient supporting evidence. Future research should prioritize transferable design patterns, benchmarks comparing integration strategies, and uncertainty-aware models that are lightweight and robust enough for online deployment in real-world settings.

</details>


### [207] [Observational Policy Ranking for SMB Financial Guidance from Multi-Action Accounting Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10050)
*Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam,Vikas Raturi,Kamalika Das,Xiang Gao,Kratika Gupta,Ruocheng Guo,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Ananya Pramod,Sricharan Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对中小企业（SMB）财务建议的排序问题，提出了一种新的方法——协变量调整残差策略学习（CAR-PL），利用85,078条公司月度观测数据（来自7,505家公司），在34个账目衍生的业务变更类别中为财务KPI选择最优策略。CAR-PL在多标签日志上直接操作，通过与多种基线方法对比，在毛利润、收入、速动比率等KPI上取得有竞争力的表现，并产生更分散的策略选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业需要及时的财务指导，但历史会计日志记录的是自我选择的、常常共同发生的业务变更，而非随机化的建议，这构成了观察性策略排名问题。缺乏一个能直接处理多热（multi-hot）日志的方法来从非随机化数据中学习最优财务建议策略。

Method: 提出CAR-PL（Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning），一种基于行动级R学习器的方法，直接在多热日志上操作，并通过观察支持度进行正则化。对比方法包括uplift T-Learner、保守上下文价值模型、零样本LLM以及非个性化参考方法，在公司不相交的保留企业上使用共享的模型辅助评分规则进行评估。

Result: CAR-PL在毛利润上获得最高点估计（0.084），T-Learner在收入上最高（0.085），上下文价值模型在速动比率上最高（0.062）。CAR-PL和T-Learner在增长类KPI上没有统计显著差异，但CAR-PL选择33-34个类别且选择更分散。结果在替换零治疗参考为最常见训练共现模式时依然稳健。

Conclusion: 多行动会计日志可以支持针对特定KPI的中小企业财务建议排序，不同目标财务KPI需要不同的策略模型，选择与结果的一致性验证了观察性策略学习的可行性和客观性。

Abstract: Small and medium-sized businesses need timely financial guidance, yet historical accounting logs record self-selected and often co-occurring business changes rather than randomized recommendations. We formulate this setting as observational policy ranking: from pre-decision financial information, a policy selects one of 34 ledger-derived business-change categories for a target financial KPI. Using 85,078 company-month observations from 7,505 firms, we introduce Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning (CAR-PL), an action-wise R-learner that operates directly on multi-hot logs and regularizes selection by observational support. We compare CAR-PL with an uplift T-Learner, a conservative contextual value model, a zero-shot LLM, and non-personalized references on company-disjoint held-out firms under a shared model-assisted scoring rule. CAR-PL has the highest Gross Profit point estimate (0.084), the T-Learner has the highest Revenue point estimate (0.085), and the contextual value model has the highest Quick Ratio point estimate (0.062). CAR-PL and the T-Learner are not statistically separated on either growth KPI in matched company-clustered comparisons, while CAR-PL selects 33-34 categories and produces less concentrated selections across the catalog. Outcome-model-only scoring retains the same KPI-level point-estimate leader or top pair, and category rankings remain similar when the all-zero treatment reference is replaced by the most common training co-action pattern. These findings support objective-specific ranking of SMB financial guidance from multi-action accounting logs.

</details>


### [208] [ChronoSSM: Training for Temporally Aware Representations in Autoregressive State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10120)
*Adrien Schoen,Nachiketa Ratnakar Patil,Arjun Bhagoji,Francesco Bronzino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoSSM，一个联合建模事件和时间戳的自回归状态空间模型，通过共享骨干网络和组合的token预测与时间生成目标进行训练，实验表明联合训练相比两阶段训练能使冻结表示中恢复出更多的时间间隔信息，且不损害内容生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型（Transformer、SSM）通常只预测事件内容而将时间视为次要信号，在带显式时间戳的数据挖掘场景中，这种分离限制了时间推理、异常检测和时间线重建。常见的两阶段训练方法隐式假设事件预测的表示已包含足够的时间结构，但这一假设缺乏验证。

Method: 提出ChronoSSM，一个自回归状态空间模型，使用共享骨干网络以组合的token生成和时间戳生成目标进行联合训练。模型直接在训练中对时间和事件进行联合建模，并与两阶段训练（先冻结事件表示再单独训练时间模型）进行系统比较，覆盖四个领域、稀疏和密集时间戳监督场景。

Result: 在四个领域、密集和部分时间戳监督条件下，联合训练一致地使冻结表示中更易恢复出到达间隔信息，同时内容生成质量没有系统性下降。表明时间监督能产生更具时间信息量的表示，而不会实质性地损害自回归事件建模。

Conclusion: 联合时间-事件建模在提升表示的时间信息可恢复性方面优于两阶段方法，且不会显著牺牲内容生成性能，为时间感知的序列建模提供了更有效的训练范式。

Abstract: Modern sequence models, from Transformers to State Space Models, have enabled powerful generative modeling across diverse domains, yet they are typically trained to predict what happens while treating when it happens as a secondary concern. In data-mining settings where events are associated with explicit timing information, this separation can limit temporal reasoning, anomaly detection, and faithful reconstruction of event chronology. A common strategy is to treat timing as an auxiliary signal, training a separate timing model using representations learned solely for event prediction. However, this two-stage approach implicitly assumes that representations optimized for event prediction already contain sufficient temporal structure.
  We introduce ChronoSSM, an autoregressive State Space Model (SSM) that jointly models events and timestamps with a shared backbone trained using combined token and temporal generation objectives. We compare the joint regime, where temporal supervision updates the backbone, with the two-stage regime, where timing is learned only using the frozen event representations. Across four domains spanning dense and partial timestamp supervision, joint training consistently makes inter-arrival information more recoverable from frozen representations without any systematic degradation in content-generation quality overall. Our results show that temporal supervision can produce more temporally informative representations without materially degrading autoregressive event modeling.

</details>


### [209] [Procedural Fairness Failures in RLHF from Preference Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10126)
*M P V S Gopinadh,Karthik Kamuju,Kummari Avinash,John Joshua,Srinivasa Raju Rudraraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出标准RLHF通过偏好平均化将异质性偏好聚合为单一奖励模型，导致多数群体主导奖励学习而少数群体偏好被系统性低估的程序性公平失败。作者提出偏好感知RLHF（PA-RLHF），在奖励学习阶段分离不同偏好模式进行优化，在受控设置中将整体对齐准确率从46.9%提升至67.9%，并将最好与最差对齐群体间的公平差距从15.9个百分点降至9.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLHF假设偏好同质性，将异质性偏好聚合为单一奖励模型，导致多数偏好群体主导奖励学习、少数偏好被系统性忽视的程序性公平失败。作者希望证明这种失败源于奖励学习的结构性设计选择，并寻求解决方案。

Method: 定义了对齐中的程序性公平概念——在奖励建模过程中保留不同的偏好信号。通过偏好平均化揭示标准RLHF对此公平性的违反。提出PA-RLHF，在奖励学习阶段将优化过程按偏好模式分离，而非将所有偏好合并到单一模型中。

Result: 在受控无噪声设置下，PA-RLHF将整体对齐准确率从46.9%提升到67.9%，同时将最好与最差对齐群体之间的公平差距从15.9个百分点缩小到9.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 程序性公平失败可能源于奖励学习的结构性设计选择，即使在受控无噪声环境下也会出现。该发现对大规模语言模型和智能体系统具有直接影响，因为偏斜的奖励模型可能在顺序决策中加剧不公平。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) aggregates heterogeneous preferences into a single reward model, assuming preference homogeneity. When preferences are heterogeneous, this aggregation induces a procedural fairness failure where majority preference groups dominate reward learning while minority preferences are systematically under-represented. This work defines procedural fairness in alignment as preserving distinct preference signals during reward modeling and shows that standard RLHF violates this via preference averaging. Preference-Aware RLHF (PA-RLHF) is introduced, separating optimization across preference modes at the reward learning stage. In a controlled setting, PA-RLHF improves overall alignment accuracy from 46.9% to 67.9% and reduces the fairness gap between best and worst aligned groups from 15.9 to 9.6 percentage points. These results show that procedural fairness failures in alignment can arise from structural design choices in reward learning, even in controlled, noise-free settings, with direct implications for large language models and agentic systems, where biased reward models can compound inequities across sequential decisions.

</details>


### [210] [SeFoRA: Sketch-Aggregated Federated Low-Rank Adaptation with Heterogeneous Client Ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10144)
*Yue Xia,Tayyebeh Jahani-Nezhad,Mayank Bakshi,Rawad Bitar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SeFoRA，一种基于草图聚合的联邦LoRA微调算法，通过传输本地更新的线性草图实现联邦端的直接聚合，缓解了双线性不匹配问题，并推导了收敛性证明及实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦参数高效微调中引入LoRA面临两个挑战：不同客户端可能使用不同的LoRA秩导致因子矩阵维度不兼容，以及因子级平均存在双线性不匹配问题。这些问题在单独的联邦学习或LoRA场景中均不存在。

Method: SeFoRA：每个客户端传输其本地更新的线性草图，联邦端直接聚合，从而缓解双线性不匹配并在全模型的小子空间中完成聚合。还提出SeFoRA-Ho（同秩版本），支持该设定下的直接适配器聚合。

Result: 对于同秩设定，证明了以O(1/T)速率收敛到一阶稳定点的邻域。在GLUE数据集上对RoBERTa-Large进行微调的数值实验表明，所提算法优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SeFoRA有效解决了联邦LoRA中不同秩不兼容及双线性不匹配问题，在理论和实验层面均验证了其优越性，为联邦参数高效微调提供了新的聚合范式。

Abstract: We consider federated parameter efficient fine-tuning of large neural networks with low-rank adaptation (LoRA,~Hu et al.\ 2022). Combining LoRA with federated PEFT introduces challenges absent from either setting alone: clients may use different LoRA ranks, making their factor matrices dimension-incompatible, and factor-wise averaging suffers from a bilinear mismatch. We propose SeFoRA, a sketch-aggregated federated LoRA algorithm in which each client transmits a linear sketch of its local updates, enabling direct aggregation at the federator. As a result, SeFoRA alleviates the bilinear mismatch, and allows for aggregation in a small subspace of the full model. We introduce a rank-homogeneous version called SeFoRA-Ho which allows for direct adapter aggregation in this setting. We prove convergence to a neighborhood of the first-order stationary point at rate $\cO(1/T)$ for the rank-homogeneous setting. Numerical experiments on fine-tuning RoBERTa-Large on GLUE datasets show how our algorithms outperform the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [211] [The Evaluation Protocol Determines the Result: An Independent Reproduction of LeWorldModel on TwoRoom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10145)
*Joyjeet Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对LeWorldModel潜世界模型进行了独立复现，发现其宣称的87%目标达成率在特定评估协议下可复现（94.0%），但需要四项未公开的配置约定。作者自带的权重在不同评估设置下结果差异巨大（8%到84%），且发现一步预测精度不能预测长期规划成功率，批归一化层会严重扭曲验证损失的报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LeWorldModel论文中宣称的潜世界模型性能是否可复现，识别复现过程中影响结果的关键配置约定，并揭示评估协议中存在的模糊性和潜在问题。

Method: 作者通过独立重实现LeWorldModel，使用约25美元的租用计算资源和单台笔记本CPU进行评估。系统性地比较了不同配置组合（包括框架步长内密集动作采集、动作编码器宽度、ImageNet像素归一化、动作z评分）对结果的影响，并测试了不同评估协议（目标偏移、步数预算）下的模型表现。

Result: 在仓库评估目标偏移下达到94.0%的成功率（高于作者自查权重的84.0%），复现了位置探针Pearson r=0.9988（报告为0.996）。但仅按公开发布配置复现时预测器无法收敛。作者权重在不同评估设置下表现从8%到84%不等，一步预测误差与长时域规划成功之间无相关性，批归一化层导致验证损失虚高最多300倍。

Conclusion: LeWorldModel的结果可在特定配置和评估协议下复现，但关键配置和评估细节未在公开发布材料中完整呈现，导致复现困难。此外，一步预测性能不能作为长时域规划能力的代理指标，论文报告中的某些指标（如验证损失）可能因批归一化层而被系统性扭曲。

Abstract: LeWorldModel trains a latent world model with a prediction loss and a single anti-collapse regulariser, and reports approximately 87% of goals reached on TwoRoom, its simplest diagnostic environment. We reproduce that result by independent reimplementation on roughly $25 of rented compute, with all evaluation on one laptop CPU.
  We reach 94.0% at the repository's evaluation goal offset, against 84.0% for the authors' own released checkpoint measured under our protocol on identical episodes, and we reproduce the reported representation result directly (position probe Pearson r = 0.9988 against a reported 0.996). Reaching that point required four conventions that determine the outcome and appear in no released configuration file: dense action gathering across a frameskip block, a programmatically-set action-encoder width, ImageNet pixel normalisation, and action z-scoring. A reproducer following the released configurations alone obtains a model whose predictor cannot converge.
  The evaluation protocol is itself contested by the released material. The paper's appendix and the repository's configuration specify different goal offsets and step budgets; on the authors' own weights these yield 14.0% and 84.0%, and only the configuration's values reproduce the reported figure. On fifty identical episodes, changing nothing but how the goal is constructed moves that checkpoint from 84.0% to 8.0%.
  Two findings generalise. One-step prediction accuracy does not predict long-horizon planning success: across three checkpoints spanning a sevenfold range in prediction error, including the authors' own, it orders short-horizon success monotonically and fails to order long-horizon success at all. And a batch normalisation layer inflated our reported validation loss by up to a factor of 300, concealing a training loss that was flat throughout.

</details>


### [212] [REATS: LLM Reasoning-based Ensemble Learning for Adaptive Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10149)
*Xu Zhang,Chang Xu,Hui Sun,Nan Ma,Zijian Zhang,Peng Wang,Wei Wang,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REATS框架，利用大语言模型的推理能力作为智能集成路由器，通过链式思维推理为时间序列预测生成可解释、样本自适应的集成权重，在八个基准测试上优于现有集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列具有多样性，单一预测模型无法在所有样本上持续最优。现有集成学习方法依赖固定规则或仅基于数值输入的黑盒模型，未能利用LLM的推理能力进行可解释的权重决策。

Method: REATS包含三大核心设计：(i) 结构化输入管线，将原始时间序列转换为混合文本-数值表示，具有固定token成本，支持无API依赖的基于规则的链式思维构建，并增强检索相似样本先验；(ii) 多样化的多行权重监督方案，配合token高效的百分比表格格式，降低数值复杂度并缓解LLM幻觉；(iii) 两阶段微调框架，结合SFT与GRPO，通过倒数奖励映射将连续无界的MSE差距转换为有界信号，放大近最优敏感度，解决朴素奖励设计中均匀敏感度和异常值主导的问题。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的实验表明，REATS优于竞争性的集成基线方法，同时能提供自然语言解释，并展现出强大的迁移学习能力和对未见候选模型的域外泛化能力。

Conclusion: REATS验证了利用LLM推理进行时间序列集成学习的可行性和有效性，通过可解释的、样本自适应的权重分配超越了传统集成方法，并为回归型GRPO奖励设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Due to the diversity of real-world time series, no single forecasting model consistently dominates across all samples. Ensemble learning addresses this by combining complementary model strengths, yet existing methods rely on fixed rules or black-box models based solely on numerical inputs, failing to leverage LLM reasoning for interpretable weighting decisions. We propose REATS, which leverages LLM reasoning capabilities as an intelligent ensemble router that jointly processes textual temporal pattern descriptions and numerical features to produce interpretable, sample-adaptive ensemble weights through chain-of-thought reasoning. To enable effective LLM-based ensembling, we study its key design choices and propose: (i) a structured input pipeline that transforms raw time series into hybrid textual--numerical representations with fixed token cost, enabling rule-based chain-of-thought construction without API dependency, augmented with retrieved similar-sample priors; (ii) a diverse multi-row weight supervision scheme coupled with a token-efficient percentage-table format that reduces numerical complexity and mitigates LLM hallucinations; and (iii) a two-stage fine-tuning framework combining SFT with GRPO, where a reciprocal reward mapping transforms the continuous unbounded MSE gap into bounded signals with amplified near-oracle sensitivity, addressing the uniform sensitivity and outlier-dominated advantage compression inherent in naive reward designs for regression-based GRPO. Experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate that REATS outperforms competitive ensemble baselines while providing natural language explanations and demonstrating strong transfer learning and out-of-domain generalization to unseen candidate models.

</details>


### [213] [Intrinsic Structure: Spectral Identifiability for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10172)
*Ashim Dhor,Pin-Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将字典学习/稀疏自编码器所发现的电路视为可辨识性问题，通过Koopman算子将前向传播提升为有限线性实现，证明其谱是模型坐标无关的固有属性，并以M^{-1/2}速率可恢复，首次为机制可解释性原语提供了可辨识性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机制可解释性通过识别模型内部电路来解释模型，但无法区分电路是模型固有属性还是方法伪影。稀疏自编码器在不同种子和宽度下会恢复出实质不同的特征，现有理论无法说明这种变异性是偶然的还是结构性的。作者希望将字典学习置于可辨识性的理论基础之上。

Method: 将前向传播视为以深度为时间维度的受控动力系统，用Koopman算子将其提升为有限线性实现，其谱（spectrum）是模型的坐标无关属性。证明谱可从M个校准样本以M^{-1/2}速率恢复（直到置换），并给出配套的极小极大下界、针对重尾激活的median-of-means变体，以及分离定理：当实现非正规时，承载激活方差的方向与跨深度承载信息的方向不能重合。

Result: 在GPT-2 small、Gemma-2-2B和Qwen3-8B-Base上，谱处处收敛，且在Qwen3-8B-Base上达到预测指数（0.506±0.031）；不足部分塌缩到一条统一曲线上。Koopman模态在间接宾语识别任务上胜过随机方向但不如主成分，差距随深度距离以4.1倍衰减，与定理预测一致。

Conclusion: Koopman谱是一个可辨识的、模型固有的指纹，带有明确的误差条，但它不是一种易读（legible）的分解。可辨识对象与易读对象不是同一个对象，这为机制可解释性提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability explains models by identifying circuits inside them, but has no way to tell whether a circuit is a property of the model or an artifact of the method that found it. Sparse autoencoders illustrate the problem: different seeds and widths recover materially different features from the same activations, and no theory says whether that variability is incidental or structural. We put dictionary learning for interpretability on an identifiability footing. Treating the forward pass as a controlled dynamical system with depth as time and lifting it with the Koopman operator yields a finite linear realisation whose \emph{spectrum} is a coordinate-free property of the model. We prove the spectrum is recoverable from $M$ calibration samples at rate $M^{-1/2}$ up to permutation - to our knowledge the first identifiability theorem for a mechanistic-interpretability primitive, with a matching minimax lower bound, a median-of-means variant for heavy-tailed activations, and a dissociation theorem: whenever the realisation is non-normal, the directions carrying activation variance and the directions carrying information across depth cannot coincide. The identifiable object and the legible object are not the same object. On GPT-2 small, Gemma-2-2B and Qwen3-8B-Base the spectrum converges everywhere and attains the predicted exponent on Qwen3-8B-Base ($0.506 \pm 0.031$); shortfalls collapse onto one curve against each cell's sample threshold. Koopman modes beat random directions but lose to principal components on indirect-object identification, with the gap decaying $4.1\times$ in depth-distance, as the theorem predicts. The Koopman spectrum is an identifiable, model-intrinsic fingerprint with a stated error bar, not a legible decomposition.

</details>


### [214] [From Prediction to Incrementality: Causal Optimization for Large-Scale Targeting and Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10182)
*Changshuai Wei,John Bencina,Phuc Nguyen,Andre Assuncao Silva T Ribeiro,Benjamin Zelditch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种决策中心的因果优化框架，将因果神经网络（Transformer骨干）、贝叶斯神经bandit层和基于对偶的大规模线性规划层统一到单一目标下，用于在全局约束下优化增量因果效应。离线模拟、架构消融和LinkedIn Feed营销的在线A/B测试验证了其有效性，最终实现主长期价值指标+7.20%的统计显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大规模推荐系统基于预测分数配合启发式或局部分配，在追求增量影响的业务场景（如营销活动、激励、通知）中，会系统性地将资源错配给那些无论如何都会行动的用户。因此需要从预测导向转向决策导向，直接优化全局约束下的因果效应。

Method: 提出一个三组件统一的决策中心框架：(1) 以Transformer为骨干的因果神经网络用于个体治疗效果估计；(2) 贝叶斯神经bandit层用于不确定性感知的探索；(3) 基于对偶的大规模线性规划层用于约束分配。框架还支持通过Transformer编码器和结果嵌入实现序列上下文以及多结果、属性条件评分。

Result: 通过公开bandit数据集的离线模拟、针对性架构消融以及LinkedIn Feed营销流量的在线A/B测试进行评估，最终端到端治疗策略在主要长期价值指标上实现了统计显著的+7.20%提升，并提炼了因果训练数据构建和成本交付控制的生产经验。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在生产规模下实现受业务约束的因果优化的可行性，为大规模推荐系统从预测优化向因果决策优化转变提供了实践验证，其生产经验对类似部署具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Large-scale targeting and recommendation systems are typically built around predictive scores fed into heuristic or local allocation. When the business goal is incremental impact, as in marketing campaigns, incentives, and notifications, this paradigm systematically misallocates resources toward users who would have acted anyway. We present a decision-centric framework that instead optimizes causal effects under global constraints, aligning three components under a single objective: a causal neural network with a Transformer backbone for individual treatment-effect estimation, a Bayesian neural-bandit layer for uncertainty-aware exploration, and a dual-based large-scale linear-programming layer for constrained allocation. The framework also supports sequential context and multi-outcome, attribute-conditioned scoring through a Transformer encoder and outcome embeddings. We evaluate it with offline simulations on a public bandit dataset, targeted architectural ablations, and an online A/B test on LinkedIn Feed marketing traffic. We also distill production lessons on causal training-data construction and cost and delivery control, which were critical to successful deployment. The end-to-end treatment policy delivered a statistically significant $+7.20\%$ lift in the primary long-term-value metric, demonstrating the feasibility of production-scale causal optimization under business constraints.

</details>


### [215] [ELMER: Evolutionary Language Model that Explores and Refines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10196)
*Matthew Siper,Ahmed Khalifa,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种进化语言模型（Evolutionary Language Model），通过在自然语言策略描述空间中搜索并编译为可执行程序，实现了对程序进化中"行为空间移动距离"的可控调节，利用oDPO优化条件语义突变，在252次固定预算进化搜索中提升了行为校准和搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序进化能够判断突变是否有帮助，但很难控制突变在行为空间中移动多远。传统语法编辑大小不可靠：小代码改动可能改变几乎所有行为，而大规模重写可能保持相同执行轨迹，缺乏对行为位移的可控性。

Method: 提出进化语言模型，在自然语言策略描述空间中搜索并将类型化程序编译为可执行代码。全量微调的Qwen3-8B模型学习三种任务条件操作：条件语义突变、自然语言到领域特定语言（GPTL）的编译、GPTL到自然语言的翻译。模型通过条件输入突变强度（低/中/高）并使用oDPO（直接偏好优化）进行微调。

Result: 在252次固定预算进化搜索中，oDPO同时提升了行为校准和有限预算搜索效率。自然语言表示达到了最高的留存适应度。分析表明条件输入（突变强度）系统性地改变了语义编辑构成，且在匹配的小到中等行为位移下，语言突变比传统方法保留了更多父代适应度。

Conclusion: 语言可以作为一种可引导的、基于执行接地（execution-grounded）的搜索表示，覆盖可执行程序空间，实现对程序进化中行为变化的可控调节。

Abstract: Program evolution can measure whether a mutation helped, but it rarely controls how far the mutation moves in behavior space. Syntactic edit size is an unreliable proxy: a small code change can alter nearly every action, while a larger rewrite can preserve the same execution trace. We introduce an Evolutionary Language Model that searches over natural-language policy descriptions and compiles typed programs for execution. A fully fine-tuned Qwen3-8B model learns three task-conditioned operations: conditional semantic mutation, natural language to domain-specific language (GPTL) compilation, and GPTL to natural language translation. The model is fine-tuned with conditional input on the mutation strength (low, medium, high) using Direct Preference Optimization (oDPO). Across 252 fixed-budget evolutionary searches, oDPO improves both behavioral calibration and finite-budget search efficiency. Natural-language attains the highest observed held-out fitness. Our analysis shows that the condition input (mutation strength) systematically changes semantic edit composition and that language mutations preserve more parent fitness at matched small-to-moderate behavioral displacement. These results show that language can serve as a steerable, execution-grounded search representation over executable program space.

</details>


### [216] [Boundary-Seeking Policy Gradient for Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10204)
*Chenhua Fan,Jiahui Zhu,Yuhang Zhang,Honghao Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Boundary-Seeking Policy Gradient (BSPG)，一种利用约束活跃时最优策略位于约束边界这一结构性事实的一阶安全强化学习算法。BSPG将更新方向分解为切向分量（在保持代价的同时改进奖励）和法向分量（将策略推向活跃边界），并推导出无需学习对偶变量的代数拉格朗日形式。理论保证包括约束残差从两侧收敛到零（有限时间内O(1/√T)界）、切向分量在边界上为奖励上升方向，以及收敛点的KKT条件；在Safety-Gymnasium导航任务中超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于梯度的约束强化学习方法虽然能保证可行性，但往往停留在可行域内部，没有利用CMDP中约束活跃时最优策略恰好位于约束边界这一线性规划结构性事实，导致次优的奖励表现。

Method: BSPG将策略更新分解为两部分：切向分量在保持代价一阶不变的同时提升奖励，法向分量通过带符号的残差驱动策略从可行侧或不可行侧逼近活跃边界。该方法推导出代数拉格朗日形式，引入诱导系数，无需学习对偶变量。

Result: 在精确梯度和给定正则条件下，约束残差从任一侧收敛到零并具有O(1/√T)有限时间界；切向分量为边界上的奖励上升方向；收敛参数序列在活跃约束集上满足KKT条件。在Safety-Gymnasium导航任务中，BSPG比基线获得更高奖励且更紧密跟踪约束边界。

Conclusion: BSPG弥补了现有方法的空白——现有分析仅证明可行性但未刻画收敛时的约束值。该工作表明利用约束边界结构可以同时提升奖励性能和约束满足度，为安全强化学习提供了新的理论视角和实用算法。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning maximizes reward subject to safety constraints. For Constrained Markov Decision Processes, the linear-programming view over occupancy measures implies that whenever the constraint is active at optimality, the optimal policy lies exactly on the constraint boundary, yet standard gradient-based methods do not exploit this structure and often settle in the feasible interior. We introduce Boundary-Seeking Policy Gradient (BSPG), a first-order method whose update combines a tangential component that improves reward while preserving cost to first order with a signed, residual-driven normal component that regulates the policy toward the active boundary from either side; the combined direction admits an algebraic Lagrangian form with an induced coefficient and no learned dual variable. Under exact gradients and stated regularity conditions, the constraint residual converges to zero from either side with a finite-horizon $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ bound, the tangential component is a reward-ascent direction on the boundary, and any convergent parameter sequence is stationary on the active constraint set, satisfying the KKT conditions when the limit is also a local maximizer over the feasible set. This complements existing analyses, which certify feasibility but do not characterize the constraint value at convergence. On a standard Safety-Gymnasium navigation task, BSPG attains higher reward while tracking the boundary more tightly than the compared baselines.

</details>


### [217] [A matched-integrator evaluation of Hamiltonian neural networks on pendulum and Kepler dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10235)
*Lenick Kemunto Nyabuto,Yae Ulrich Gaba,Birahim Tewe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过严格的对照实验（相同的RK4生成轨迹、相同的中心差分导数目标、相同的优化设置、相同的推理积分器）比较了哈密顿神经网络（HNN）与参数匹配的前馈基线网络在非线性摆和三维开普勒二体问题上的长时程预测性能。结果显示HNN在能量漂移、轨迹MSE和种子间变异性方面均显著优于基线，且这种优势在更具非线性的相空间区域更加明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然HNN已被提出用于参数化保守动力学，但其优于通用向量场神经网络的优势是否来自于哈密顿架构先验，还是在先前研究中由于训练协议、积分器或超参数不匹配造成的混淆，缺乏受控的公平评估。

Method: 采用受控实验协议：HNN与参数匹配的前馈基线使用相同的RK4生成轨迹、相同的中心差分导数目标、相同的优化设置，推理时使用相同的RK4积分器，并在5个独立随机种子下报告结果。对非线性摆系统进行能量分层分析，并额外考察Störmer-Verlet风格滚动的诊断，以及三维开普勒二体问题的验证。

Result: 在非线性摆上，HNN将平均能量漂移降低42倍，平均轨迹MSE降低15.8倍（T=100，约16个摆周期），能量漂移有界且种子间变异性显著低于基线。能量分层分析显示差异在非线性区域更显著。在三维开普勒二体问题上，HNN同样在轨迹、能量和角动量漂移方面优于基线。

Conclusion: 哈密顿参数化在长时程预测和物理一致性方面确实提供了显著的架构先验优势，该优势在严格的受控对比下仍然成立，并且在高非线性区域和更高维系统中表现更加突出。然而，非可分形式的哈密顿量意味着标准辛积分器的理论保证不直接适用。

Abstract: Hamiltonian Neural Networks (HNNs) parameterize conservative dynamics through a learned scalar Hamiltonian, providing an architectural prior that is absent from generic vector-field neural networks. We evaluate this prior under a controlled protocol in which an HNN and a parameter-matched feedforward baseline are trained on the same RK4-generated trajectories, use the same central-difference derivative targets and optimization settings, and are integrated at inference with the same RK4 scheme. Results are reported over five independent training seeds.
  On the nonlinear pendulum, the HNN reduces mean energy drift by 42-fold and mean trajectory MSE by 15.8-fold at T = 100, approximately 16 pendulum periods. Its energy drift also remains bounded and exhibits substantially lower seed-to-seed variability than the standard-network baseline. An energy-stratified analysis shows that the difference becomes more pronounced as trajectories explore more nonlinear regions of phase space.
  As an additional diagnostic, we examine an explicit Störmer--Verlet-style rollout of the learned HNN. Because the learned Hamiltonian is not constrained to the separable form H(q,p) = T(p) + V(q), the standard symplecticity guarantee of velocity Verlet does not directly apply.
  We further apply the same matched-integrator protocol to the three-dimensional Kepler two-body problem. The HNN again exhibits lower trajectory, energy, and angular-momentum drift than the parameter-matched baseline. These experiments provide a controlled study of how Hamiltonian parameterization affects long-horizon prediction and physical consistency across two conservative dynamical systems.

</details>


### [218] [STCAD: Scalable Trajectory Clustering and Anomaly Detection on Terabyte-Scale AIS Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10249)
*Bertram Hage,Alexander Schiøtz,Felix Thomsen,Christian Rand,Peder Heiselberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展的无监督海事轨迹聚类框架，基于BERT编码器和CURE层次聚类处理TB级AIS数据，并通过重建损失与聚类噪声分配实现异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事轨迹数据规模庞大（TB级AIS档案），现有方法难以高效处理如此大规模数据，且通常需要预定义聚类数量，缺乏可扩展且无需预设参数的无监督聚类方案。

Method: 采用自定义BERT模型对变长轨迹进行编码，通过掩码token建模进行训练；使用CURE层次聚类算法对编码后的轨迹进行聚类，无需预设聚类数；基于重建损失和聚类噪声分配设计内在无监督异常检测方法。

Result: 在国家尺度、包含数十亿条消息且跨越一年的AIS数据集上验证，得到稳定的轨迹聚类结果，并能清晰区分正常与异常的船舶行为。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在大规模真实海事数据上实现高效、稳定的无监督轨迹聚类，同时提供有效的异常航行模式识别能力。

Abstract: We present a scalable framework for unsupervised clustering of maritime trajectories derived from terabyte-scale Automatic Identification System (AIS) archives. Variable-length trajectories are encoded with a custom BERT-based model trained via masked token modeling and clustered using CURE hierarchical clustering, producing physically interpretable trajectory groups without requiring a predefined number of clusters. An intrinsic unsupervised anomaly detection method based on reconstruction loss and clustering noise assignment identifies irregular navigation patterns. The framework is demonstrated on a national-scale AIS dataset comprising billions of messages spanning one year, yielding stable trajectory clusters and a clear separation between nominal and anomalous vessel behavior.

</details>


### [219] [CRHT: A Continuous Regression Hybrid Transformer for Vessel Trajectory Prediction with Online Cluster Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10256)
*Alexander Schiøtz,Bertram Hage,Christian Rand,Felix Thomsen,Peder Heiselberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出连续回归混合Transformer（CRHT）框架，利用AIS数据预测船舶轨迹，通过在线K-means聚类采样策略解决空间数据不平衡问题，混合架构结合一维卷积和多头注意力机制，在短期（1小时）预测中取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有船舶轨迹预测模型存在地理偏差和导航真实性问题，难以满足海事安全和异常检测的实际需求。

Method: 采用在线K-means聚类采样策略缓解空间数据不平衡；构建混合架构，集成一维卷积层提取局部运动学特征，结合多头注意力机制捕获全局时序上下文。

Result: CRHT在1小时短期预测中取得最低误差；离散模型在长时预测中表现出更高的导航稳定性，而CRHT在精度和机动跟踪之间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: CRHT在实时海事监控场景中能兼顾预测精度与机动跟踪能力，适用于短期预测需求，与离散模型在不同时间尺度上各有优势。

Abstract: Accurate vessel trajectory prediction is critical for maritime safety and anomaly detection, yet existing models often struggle with geographic bias and navigational realism. We propose the Continuous Regression Hybrid Transformer (CRHT), a deep learning framework designed to forecast vessel motion using Automatic Identification System (AIS) data. To mitigate spatial data imbalance, we introduce an online K-means cluster sampling strategy that ensures diverse exposure to rare maneuvers during training. Our hybrid architecture integrates 1D convolutional layers for local kinematic feature extraction with a multi-head attention mechanism for global temporal context. CRHT demonstrates superior performance in short-term forecasting, achieving the lowest errors at the 1-hour horizon. The results demonstrate that while discrete models provide high navigational stability over long horizons, CRHT offers an optimal balance of precision and maneuver tracking for real-time maritime surveillance.

</details>


### [220] [Toward Human Rights Benchmarking for LLMs: A Pilot Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10268)
*Savannah Thais,Wm. Matthew Kennedy,Abhigyan Acherjee,Matilda Wysocki,Malcolm Langford,Caitlin Kraft Buchman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HumRightsBench，这是首个经过专家验证的、基于场景的国际人权法推理评估基准。该基准改编了IRAC法律推理框架（用"提出补救措施"P替代"法律结论"C，形成IRAP），并构建了由全球人权律师和专业人士注释的真实场景数据集。实验结果显示模型准确率在0.339到0.577之间，证明该基准能够有效评估大语言模型在人权法推理方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地介入涉及人权实现的法律裁决，但目前缺乏一个评估基准来检验模型是否正确推理人权法。这一空白可能导致模型在人权相关决策中出现错误推理，因此亟需一个稳健、可扩展的评估工具。

Method: 开发了HumRightsBench基准，采用以下方法：(1) 改编IRAC法律推理框架为IRAP（以"提议补救措施"P替代"法律结论"C），以适应人权工作的独特推理模式；(2) 构建真实场景的试点系列，涵盖现实人权问题的多维度；(3) 由全球人权律师和专业人士进行专家注释验证。

Result: 模型总体准确率范围为0.339至0.577，任务级别的最小最大范围从0.025到0.774。不同模型在不同法律推理任务上的表现差异显著，表明该基准能够有效区分模型能力。

Conclusion: HumRightsBench的表现差异强烈暗示它是一个能够推动人工智能评估科学这一新兴子领域发展的重要工具，尤其是在人权法推理评估的关键时期。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly mediate legal determinations over what human rights are realized, and how. Yet, no evaluation benchmark exists to assess whether they can reason correctly about human rights law. To this end, we report our efforts to develop a robust and scalable methodology for creating HumRightsBench: the first expert-validated, scenario-based benchmark for evaluating reasoning grounded in the obligation structure of international human rights law. We adapt the IRAC framework for legal reasoning to better suit the unique reasoning patterns of human rights work (substituting P, "proposing remedies," for C, "legal conclusion," yielding IRAP) to structure our evaluation heuristics. We also produce a pilot series of authentic scenarios designed to implicate the many dimensions of real-world human rights issues and annotated by human rights lawyers and professionals across the world. Ultimately, we find that model accuracy scores range considerably across legal reasoning tasks (overall model performance ranges from 0.339 to 0.577, task min-max ranges from 0.025 to 0.774), which strongly implies that HumRightsBench is a capable instrument for advancing this emerging subfield of AI evaluations science at a critical moment in its evolution.

</details>


### [221] [Power law graph attention: exact generalization of scaled dot-product attention, empirical collapse at inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10288)
*Burc Gokden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PLDR-LLM（基于幂律解码器表示的大语言模型），其核心是将缩放点积注意力（SDPA）中的固定双线性形式替换为学习得到的、由输入生成的$G_{LM}$双线性算子。该架构有完整规范，并区分了定理、条件定理、测量和猜想等不同层级的主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的SDPA使用固定的双线性形式，限制了注意力机制的表达能力。作者希望通过引入幂律驱动的输入生成算子来增强注意力机制，同时保持理论可验证性和数学严谨性，以构建更强大的语言模型架构。

Method: PLGA用输入生成的双线性算子$G_{LM}$（由正张量$A_{LM}$按元素幂律构建）替换SDPA的固定双线性形式；引入DAG正则器（NOTEARS形式）；提出非共振条件下的交换子判据；通过推理坍缩定理、测量的不变性、条件三阶段机制（旋转变换、浓度、行映射收缩）以及块级训练评分等手段对架构进行全面分析。

Result: 无条件下PLGA在$G_{LM}=I$时精确包含SDPA；$A_{LM}$严格逐元素为正且具有Perron-Frobenius结构；推理坍缩定理表明精确输入不变性将推理坍缩为带常数算子的广义SDPA；测量到$10^{-6}$级别的相对波动；块级与顺序评分在TruthfulQA指标上一致（每项误差在$5\times10^{-5}$内）；部分证明核在Lean 4中通过机器验证。

Conclusion: PLDR-LLM在理论上严格界定，实验上可验证，将自组织临界性作为现象学框架引入，将开放性主张转化为可证伪的猜想。尽管评估代理与解码裕度存在差距，但块级与顺序评分的一致性支持该架构的有效性。

Abstract: The Large Language Model from Power Law Decoder Representations (PLDR-LLM) and its attention, Power Law Graph Attention (PLGA), replace the fixed bilinear form of scaled dot-product attention (SDPA) with a learned, input-generated bilinear operator $G_{LM}$, built from a positive tensor $A_{LM}$ by elementwise power laws. The architecture is fully specified, verified against pinned reference releases; claims are labeled theorem, conditional theorem, measurement, or conjecture. Unconditionally: PLGA contains SDPA exactly at $G_{LM}=I$; $A_{LM}$ and $A_P$ are strictly entrywise positive, with Perron-Frobenius structure on $A_{LM}$; the DAG regularizer has the NOTEARS walk-counting form and positivity obstructs exact acyclicity; and, under nonresonance (satisfied by standard rotary frequencies), a commutant criterion identifies which operators preserve relative-position dependence. An inference-collapse theorem: exact input invariance of deductive outputs collapses inference to generalized SDPA with a constant operator. Measured invariance: relative fluctuations of $10^{-6}$ and below; perturbation bounds quantify but do not certify cached inference; the assembled proxy misses the decoding margin. A conditional three-stage mechanism (rotary twirl, concentration, row-map contraction) is measured on a released checkpoint. Blockwise training and scoring under the global Gram are stated with explicit target exposure; on tested samples, block and sequential scoring select identical answers and agree on the published TruthfulQA probability-mass metric within $5\times 10^{-5}$ per item. Self-organized criticality enters as a phenomenological framework with an intrinsic order parameter; open claims become falsifiable conjectures. Selected proof cores are machine-checked in Lean 4.

</details>


### [222] [MERA: Model Evolution and Routing with Skill Adaptation for Agentic Systems at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10333)
*Yuhang Yao,Zeyu Wang,Wanyi Chen,Tongyun Yang,Yuhang Han,Jie Xiao,Chengke Bao,Tianyi Zhao,Lynn Ai,Eric Yang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MERA提出了一种以单次模型调用为适应单元的多轮适应框架，通过重放失败的学生调用获取教师演示、将重复过程蒸馏到技能书（SkillBook）并微调学生LoRA适配器，同时结合成本校准的路由和验证器回退机制进行部署。实验表明四轮适应将Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B的通过率从28.7%提升至49.7%，在验证器回退下以60.8%的成本保留88.3%的通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由方法仅将简单调用分配给小模型、困难调用分配给大模型，以减少推理成本，但小模型自身能力没有提升，因此可节省的成本受限于学生模型已经能解决的问题范围。作者旨在通过提升小模型本身的能力来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: MERA以单次模型调用作为适应单元：每轮周期中，重放失败的学生调用以获得执行验证的教师演示，将重复出现的流程蒸馏到迭代更新的SkillBook中，并通过监督学习和可选的GRPO微调学生LoRA适配器。部署时，改进后的学生模型置于成本校准的路由器之后，配备验证器回退机制，且候选SkillBook、适配器或路由器仅在联合重放保持任务质量时才被采纳。

Result: 四轮适应使Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B在HumanEval+MBPP上的通过率从28.7%提升至49.7%；在验证器回退下，部署策略以60.8%的always-Luna成本保留88.3%的通过率；在TAU-2上，微调的Qwen3.5-2B从14/35提升至18/35，并与未适应的4B模型持平。

Conclusion: 验证器支持的多轮适应能够提升小模型自身能力，而不仅仅是在固定学生模型周围进行路由，从而在保持任务质量的同时显著降低推理成本。

Abstract: LLM agents execute heterogeneous sequences of model calls within a single task: some invocations require careful reasoning, while others are structured steps such as formatting or tool-argument construction. Prior routing methods exploit this asymmetry by assigning easy invocations to a cheaper small model and difficult ones to a large model. Such policies reduce inference cost, but they leave the small model's capability unchanged, so attainable savings remain bounded by the work the student can already solve. MERA instead improves the small model itself, using a single model invocation as the unit of adaptation. In each cycle, MERA replays failed student invocations to obtain execution-verified teacher demonstrations, distills recurring procedures into an iteratively updated SkillBook, and fine-tunes a student LoRA adapter via supervised learning and optional GRPO. Routing serves as supporting machinery for deployment: the improved student is served behind a cost-calibrated router with verifier-backed fallback, and a candidate SkillBook, adapter, or router is admitted only when joint replay preserves task quality. Empirically, four-cycle adaptation raises Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B from 28.7% to 49.7% pass on held-out HumanEval+MBPP. Under verifier-backed fallback, the deployed policy retains 88.3% pass at 60.8% of always-Luna cost. On TAU-2, a fine-tuned Qwen3.5-2B improves from 14/35 to 18/35 and matches an unadapted 4B model. These results indicate that verifier-backed multi-cycle adaptation can increase small-model capability, rather than only routing around a fixed student.

</details>


### [223] [Accelerated Learning of High Dimensional Functions with a Tensor-Featured Training Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10351)
*Karl Pierce,Yuehaw Khoo,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种加速高维函数学习的深度神经网络优化方法，通过在第一层引入上下文特征（包括快速求值的秩1特征和需张量网络分解的张量特征），交替优化DNN参数和特征层，并利用随机化张量分解将预训练DNN蒸馏为张量网络，将存储成本降低至少8个数量级，有效支持5-40维的模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维函数的深度神经网络优化通常面临计算成本高昂和存储开销巨大的挑战，亟需一种能够加速优化过程并降低资源消耗的方法。

Method: 该方法将上下文特征引入DNN第一层，在固定输入特征基的前提下用标准梯度下降优化DNN参数；优化完成后更新特征层再继续DNN优化。特征层包含两类：可矩阵无关快速求值的秩1特征，以及需通过张量网络（TN）分解策略处理的张量特征。对于从离散化DNN构建的高维函数，采用随机化张量分解策略高效分解。

Result: 随机化策略将高维函数分解的存储成本降低至少8个数量级，该方法能高效训练5至40维的模型。

Conclusion: 提出的交替优化框架结合张量网络分解与随机化技术，有效克服了高维DNN优化的计算与存储瓶颈，扩展了DNN在高维问题上的可应用范围。

Abstract: In this work we present a method to accelerate the optimization of learning high dimensional functions using deep neural network (DNN). This optimization procedure introduces contextual features into the first layer of a DNN. The parameters of DNN are optimized via standard gradient descent while keeping the input-feature basis fixed. After optimization of the DNN parameters, the feature layer is provided a chance to update and change before DNN optimization resumes. The feature layer has two types of functions: those that can be evaluated quickly in a matrix-free way on the domain (i.e. rank-1 features) and more complex features that must first be decomposed using tensor network (TN) decomposition strategies (tensor features). In particular, we study the effect of adding features which distill pretrained DNN into TNs using a discretize and decompose strategy. To efficiently decompose high-dimensional functions constructed from discretized DNN, we leverage a randomized tensor decomposition strategy. Using randomization, we are able to reduce the storage cost of decomposing high dimensional functions by at least 8 orders of magnitude. Using this approach, we are able to efficiently train models between 5 and 40 dimensions.

</details>


### [224] [Efficient Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10357)
*Zelei Cheng,Amritansh Mishra,Sambit Sahu,William Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SINKFLEX-RL，一个面向长视野工具使用智能体的模块化强化学习训练系统，结合Gymnasium环境封装、VERL风格rollout数据流、无价值模型的组相对策略优化（GRPO）以及sink感知的FlexAttention路径。实验表明，在Tau2Bench零售任务中验证奖励从0.25提升至0.44，在内存基准测试中峰值VRAM从28.06GB降至22.52GB（降低19.7%），并能运行基线方法内存不足的8192 token配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野工具使用智能体面临多轮on-policy rollout带来的长上下文问题，而模型特定的注意力层需要自定义掩码和sink归一化处理。现有方法在训练这类智能体时面临内存和系统集成方面的挑战。

Method: SINKFLEX-RL包含四个核心组件：(1) Gymnasium兼容的环境封装器，(2) VERL风格的rollout数据流，(3) 无独立价值模型的组相对策略优化（GRPO），以及(4) 保持模型特定sink缩放的FlexAttention路径，支持因果掩码和滑动窗口掩码。

Result: 在Tau2Bench零售实验中，验证奖励（mean@1）从训练早期的0.25上升到观测窗口后期的0.44；训练得分和轨迹奖励代理指标也呈上升趋势。在固定配置内存基准中，优化的注意力路径将4096 token配置的峰值VRAM从28.06GB降至22.52GB（19.7%减少），并在8192 token配置下仅用25.53GB运行，而eager基线已内存不足。

Conclusion: 将环境接口、RL数据流和注意力内核设计三者集成，对实现内存可行的长视野智能体训练具有重要价值，SINKFLEX-RL为这一领域提供了有效的系统级解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon tool-using agents must reason over user goals, domain policies, tool calls, simulator state, and delayed verifiable rewards. Reinforcement learning (RL) is a natural fit for this setting, but multi-turn on-policy rollouts create long contexts, while model-specific attention layers may require custom masks and learned sink normalization. We present SINKFLEX-RL, a modular training system for RL in dual-control tool-use environments. The system combines a Gymnasium-compatible environment wrapper, a VERL-style rollout dataflow, group-relative policy optimization without a separate value model, and a sink-aware FlexAttention path designed to preserve model-specific sink scaling under causal and sliding-window masks. In a preliminary Tau2Bench retail run, validation reward (mean@1) rises from 0.25 early in training to $0.44$ later in the observed training window, while training-score and trajectory-reward proxies also trend upward. In a fixed-configuration memory benchmark, the optimized attention path reduces peak VRAM from 28.06GB to 22.52GB at 4096 tokens, a $19.7\%$ reduction, and runs the measured 8192-token configuration using $25.53$~GB where the eager baseline runs out of memory. These results illustrate the value of integrating environment interfaces, RL dataflow, and attention-kernel design for memory-feasible long-horizon agent training.

</details>


### [225] [Invertible Logits Transformation for Accuracy-Preserving Post-Hoc Uncertainty Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10372)
*Lening Zhao,Qipeng Zhan,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Invertible Logits Transformation (InvLT)，通过对softmax前的logits逐元素应用一个共享的标量MLP进行后处理校准，参数数量与类别数C无关，并通过配对逆网络软性鼓励单调性以保持原始预测，在多个图像分类基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后处理校准方法存在明显缺陷：温度缩放表达力不足，更灵活的参数化方法参数随类别数线性增长，而其他表达力强的方法不能保持类别分数的排序顺序，可能改变预测类别。需要一个同时满足非线性校正能力、可扩展到大规模标签空间、保留原始预测三个特性的校准器。

Method: InvLT学习一个标量MLP f: R→R，将其逐元素应用于softmax前的logits。由于f在所有logit维度间共享，参数量与类别数C无关。通过配对逆网络软性鼓励f的单调性（而非像先前方法那样通过数值积分强制实现），从而避免计算开销，同时经验上保持原始分类精度。

Result: 在标准图像分类基准和多种架构上，InvLT在标准校准指标上持续优于广泛的现有后处理校准基线方法，并在所有测试场景中经验性地保持了原始分类准确率。

Conclusion: InvLT通过共享标量MLP加配对逆网络的设计，兼顾了非线性校正能力、与类别数无关的参数量以及预测保留特性，为后处理校准提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-hoc calibration aligns a classifier's predicted confidences with its empirical accuracy without retraining. An ideal calibrator should correct nonlinear miscalibration, scale gracefully to large label spaces, and preserve the original predictions; existing methods typically violate at least one of these properties---temperature scaling lacks expressivity, more flexible parametric alternatives introduce parameters that grow with the number of classes $C$, and other expressive methods do not preserve the rank ordering of class scores and may alter the predicted class. We propose \textbf{Invertible Logits Transformation (InvLT)}, which applies a learned scalar MLP $f:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}$ element-wise to the pre-softmax logits. Sharing $f$ across all logit dimensions makes the parameter count independent of $C$. Monotonicity of $f$---and hence preservation of the argmax prediction---is softly encouraged via a paired inverse network rather than enforced through the numerical integration required by prior monotone calibrators; this avoids their computational overhead while empirically preserving the original classification accuracy in every setting we evaluate. Across standard image classification benchmarks and a range of architectures, InvLT consistently outperforms a broad set of post-hoc baselines on standard calibration metrics.

</details>


### [226] [Fisher8: Stabilizing Neural Heteroscedastic Regression via Output-Layer Fisher Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10374)
*Sumedh Vemuganti,Nickvash Kani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fisher8，一种基于Fisher几何而非欧氏几何的输出层梯度校正方法，用于稳定联合预测均值和不确定性的神经网络训练，在回归和表征学习任务中取得更优的似然-误差权衡和校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络联合预测均值和不确定性估计时训练不稳定，虽已有多种独立的稳定化干预措施，但现有方法缺乏统一的视角来解释为何不稳定以及如何系统性解决。

Method: 作者认为现有稳定化干预措施指向一个共同的潜在问题——梯度步长与损失景观的几何形状对齐不良。为此，他们提出Fisher8，一种输出层梯度校正方法，利用Fisher几何而非欧氏几何重新定向和缩放梯度更新，使更新更好地与局部曲率对齐。该方法不引入除学习率外的数据相关超参数，并允许在连续预测分布之间近似KL信任半径。

Result: 在多维回归和表征学习任务中，Fisher8取得更优的似然-误差权衡，预测出校准良好的不确定性估计，并学习到丰富的不确定性感知特征空间。此外，研究表明先前的稳定化方法收敛于该几何校正的重叠组件上。

Conclusion: Fisher8提供了一个统一且简单有效的解决方案，将先前的独立稳定化努力整合到一致的几何框架下，为不确定性估计的联合训练提供了更稳健和理论上有依据的途径。

Abstract: Training neural networks to jointly predict mean and uncertainty estimates from noisy observations can be unstable, prompting a series of independent stabilization efforts. We argue that these interventions highlight a common underlying issue where gradient steps are poorly aligned with the geometry of the loss landscape. To better align updates with local curvature, we derive Fisher8, an output-layer gradient correction that reorients and rescales updates using Fisher geometry rather than Euclidean geometry. Unlike past stabilizers, Fisher8 introduces no data-dependent hyperparameters beyond learning rate and admits an approximate KL trust radius between successive predictive distributions. We show that prior stabilizers converge on overlapping components of this geometric correction. Across multidimensional regression and representation-learning tasks, Fisher8 obtains superior likelihood--error tradeoffs, predicts calibrated uncertainty estimates, and learns rich uncertainty-aware feature spaces.

</details>


### [227] [Generator-Guided Inverse Sampling for Lévy-Driven Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10384)
*Tianfu Qi,Jun Wang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从马尔可夫生成元角度研究Lévy驱动的生成模型的逆向采样问题。由于Lévy过程含无限跳活动，其逆过程是非局部的，不能仅靠分数信息刻画。作者推导出逆向跳分量是状态依赖的马尔可夫跳过程，由非局部密度比控制，并据此设计了将扩散、小跳和大跳分量分解的结构化逆向采样器。针对各向同性线性Lévy SDE，开发了计算可行的采样算法，神经网络仅用于摊销大跳速率，跳幅由解析推导的分布生成。该采样器还适用于观测引导采样，并在OFDM-SISO信道估计中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型的逆向采样依赖于分数匹配，但Lévy驱动的生成模型包含无限跳活动，其逆向过程是非局部的，难以仅用分数信息刻画，因此需要新的理论框架和采样方法。

Method: 通过分析前向和逆向马尔可夫生成元，推导逆向跳分量为状态依赖的马尔可夫跳过程，由非局部密度比控制。基于此构建分解为扩散、小跳和大跳分量的结构化逆向采样器；对对称α稳定跳的各向同性线性Lévy SDE，神经网络仅用于摊销大跳速率，跳幅由解析条件分布生成，并引入高效实现技术避免高维积分和采样。

Result: 在OFDM-SISO信道估计任务中，该采样器在混合高斯和脉冲噪声下表现出稳健的估计性能，且实现了复杂度与性能的良好权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为Lévy驱动的生成模型提供了理论上有据可依且计算上可行的逆向采样框架，通过分解动力学分量提升了模型的可解释性和可控性，并在实际通信场景中验证了其应用价值。

Abstract: This paper studies inverse sampling for Lévy-driven generative models from the perspective of Markov generators. Unlike conventional diffusion models, Lévy-driven dynamics involve infinite jump activities, which makes their reverse process nonlocal and difficult to characterize using score information alone. We address this challenge by analyzing the forward and reversed generators. It is derived that the reversed jump component generally becomes a state-dependent Markov jump process governed by a nonlocal density ratio. This observation motivates a structured reverse sampler that decomposes the dynamics into diffusion, small jump, and large jump components. Based on this characterization, we develop a computationally tractable sampler for a class of isotropic linear Lévy SDEs with symmetric $α$-stable jump components. For the jump component, the neural network is used only to amortize the rate of large jump activities, while jump amplitudes are generated from analytically derived conditional distributions, which improves interpretability and controllability. Efficient implementation techniques are further introduced under this setting to avoid expensive high-dimensional integration and sampling. The sampler is further adapted to approximate observation-guided sampling and applied to OFDM-SISO channel estimation under mixed Gaussian and impulsive noise. Simulations show robust estimation performance with a favorable tradeoff between complexity and performance.

</details>


### [228] [Dreamer-SAC: Off-Policy Learning in Latent World Models for Sample-Efficient Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10386)
*Jiazhuo Li,Linjiang Cao,Qi Liu,Xi Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dreamer-SAC框架，将循环状态空间世界模型与软演员-评论家算法结合，在潜在空间中直接进行策略优化，通过真实交互与短视界生成轨迹的混合训练，在自动驾驶场景中显著提升样本效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的样本高效强化学习常受限于数据效率与模型偏差之间的权衡。虽然世界模型减少了环境交互的依赖，但基于学习动态的策略优化对预测误差敏感，需要一种既能充分利用世界模型数据又对模型偏差鲁棒的训练方法。

Method: 提出Dreamer-SAC框架，集成循环状态空间世界模型与离策略软演员-评论家算法，直接在潜在空间训练。采用真实交互与短视界生成轨迹的组合，配合n步目标估计与多目标监督，兼顾驾驶效率与安全性。

Result: 在自动驾驶场景中，Dreamer-SAC在驾驶效率与安全性方面一致优于DreamerV3、SAC和PPO等基线，且使用更少的真实环境交互即可获得更好的性能。实验发现滚动视界与策略性能呈倒U型关系，短视界潜在滚动达到最佳权衡；n步目标估计优于一步时序差分目标。

Conclusion: 短视界潜在滚动与n步目标估计是实现样本高效且鲁棒的自动驾驶强化学习的关键设计选择，Dreamer-SAC在性能与数据效率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Sample-efficient reinforcement learning for autonomous driving is often limited by the trade-off between data efficiency and model bias. While world models reduce the reliance on costly environment interactions, policy optimization over learned dynamics remains sensitive to prediction errors. This paper proposes the Dreamer-SAC framework, which integrates a recurrent state-space world model with an off-policy soft actor-critic algorithm trained directly in latent space. The framework uses a combination of real interactions and short-horizon generated trajectories with n-step target estimation and multi-objective supervision. Evaluated in autonomous driving scenarios with objectives encompassing driving efficiency and safety, the proposed framework consistently outperforms representative reinforcement learning baselines, including DreamerV3, SAC, and PPO, while achieving improved performance with substantially fewer real environment interactions. Experiments reveal an inverted-U relationship between rollout horizon and policy performance, where short-horizon latent rollouts achieve the best trade-off between additional training signals and accumulated model bias. Furthermore, n-step target estimation demonstrates more effectiveness over one-step temporal-difference targets in exploiting predicted experience for value learning.

</details>


### [229] [Share First, Route What Remains: A Unified Framework for Token-Adaptive MoE Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10392)
*Gongli Zhang,Zhulin Liu,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UniF-MoE框架，基于"先共享、再路由"的原则统一了MoE中的共享专家、细粒度计算和动态路由机制，通过分解专家为键值通道并利用共享需求分数、关键值原型和Gram正则化，实现了更好的性能和更低的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型中的共享专家设计、细粒度计算方法和动态路由机制通常是独立决策的，忽略了它们之间的基本依赖关系——提取可复用计算会同时改变剩余部分的内容和所需专家容量。

Method: 将稀疏上循环的前馈专家分解为键值通道；观察共激活专家在值位置的子集上对齐，移除这些位置会改变专家偏好，且更大的共享覆盖与更低的剩余专家需求相关；据此提出UniF-MoE统一框架，每个专家划分为对齐块，共享需求分数决定共享块数和路径权重，键原型选择共享内容，累积路由质量决定剩余专家数，并使用Gram正则化器分离和归一化路由器嵌入。

Result: 在DomainBed和GLUE基准上的实验表明，UniF-MoE相比代表性的静态和动态MoE方法在预测性能上更优，同时减少了激活计算量、推理延迟和内存占用。

Conclusion: 研究证明了共享与路由之间的依赖关系对MoE设计至关重要，"先共享、再路由"的统一原则能够同时提升性能与效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models have recently moved beyond routing a fixed number of complete experts. Shared-expert designs preserve reusable knowledge, fine-grained methods vary computation within experts, and dynamic routers adapt the number of active experts. Yet these decisions are usually made independently, overlooking a basic dependency: extracting reusable computation changes both what remains and how much expert capacity the remainder needs. We study this dependency by decomposing sparsely upcycled feed-forward experts into key-value channels. Co-activated experts align at a subset of value positions; removing these positions changes expert preference; and greater shared coverage is associated with lower residual expert demand. These observations lead to one principle: share first, then route what remains. We instantiate it in UniF-MoE, a unified framework for token-adaptive MoE computation. Each expert is partitioned into aligned blocks. A shared-demand score sets the shared block count and pathway weight, key prototypes select the shared content, and the complementary demand determines the residual expert count through cumulative routing mass. A Gram regularizer separates and normalizes router embeddings, promoting diverse routing directions, sparse expert overlap, and a simple routing geometry. Experiments on DomainBed and GLUE show that this unified design improves predictive performance over representative static and dynamic MoEs while reducing activated computation, inference latency, and memory. Code is available at https://github.com/existence0420/UniF-MoE.

</details>


### [230] [ELVAE: Evidential Learning-Based Variational Autoencoder for Uncertainty-Aware Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10398)
*Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ELVAE，一种基于证据学习的变分自编码器，每个潜变量坐标由输入相关的正态-逆伽马后验控制，从而显式区分潜位置的不确定性与周围的变异性，并利用这种不确定性指导生成过程（如低不确定性用于可靠合成，高不确定性用于压力测试）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VAE仅从概率潜表示中采样，但无法区分潜位置的不确定性与潜空间本身的变异性，这限制了对生成结果语义可靠性的控制和理解。

Method: 通过证据学习框架，使每个潜变量坐标由输入相关的正态-逆伽马后验支配，形成完整层级结构，并推导出精确的ELBO目标函数；同时证明必须直接正则化整个层级，因为仅对边缘化潜分布进行正则化无法识别不确定性分解。

Result: 在MNIST生成实验中，冻结外部分类器条件下，该不确定性能够有效分层生成数字的语义可靠性；零位移对照实验表明效果主要来自锚点可再生成性的可靠性，另有较小但独立的不确定性缩放扰动成分；且仅在同类别内不确定性排名下效果显著，强度随随机种子变化。

Conclusion: 学习到的潜位置不确定性可作为实用的控制变量用于不确定性感知生成，能够将锚点可靠性与扰动引发的失败分离开来。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders generate samples from probabilistic latent representations but do not distinguish uncertainty about the latent location from variability around it. We formulate ELVAE, an evidential learning-based VAE in which each latent coordinate is governed by an input-dependent normal-inverse-gamma posterior. This hierarchy yields an explicit latent-location uncertainty that can be used during generation, not merely reported after inference: low-uncertainty anchors support more reliable synthetic samples, while high-uncertainty anchors can be deliberately exploited for stress testing. The objective is an exact evidence lower bound, and we show that direct regularization of the full hierarchy is required, since the marginalized latent law alone cannot identify the uncertainty decomposition. In an MNIST generation pilot with a frozen external classifier, this uncertainty clearly stratified the semantic reliability of generated digits. A zero-displacement control revealed that most of the effect reflects how reliably an anchor can be re-generated, while a smaller but distinct component is attributable to uncertainty-scaled perturbation itself. The effect holds only under within-class uncertainty ranking, and its magnitude varies across seeds. These findings support the learned latent-location uncertainty as a practical control variable for uncertainty-aware generation, separating anchor reliability from perturbation-induced failure.

</details>


### [231] [Do Judges Behave Like Algorithms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10400)
*Riya Manchanda,Eric Chen,Chloe Zhu,Cynthia Rudin,Brandon Garrett,Songman Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析德克萨斯州哈里斯县轻罪保释听证会的司法决策，运用机器学习模型探索法官是否以类似算法的可预测规则行事。研究发现法官大体上确实遵循可解释的小型公式进行决策，但在某些案件中法官之间存在显著差异，导致类似被告受到不一致或不平等的对待。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能和算法广泛部署于司法系统的背景下，作者提出一个反向问题：法官是否已经像算法一样行事？如果法官的决策遵循基于离散、静态因素（如犯罪记录、年龄、指控类型）的一致规则，则司法行为可以被改进；反之，如果依赖无法从法庭数据中识别的个体化信息，则基于标准的决策将更难被理解和优化。

Method: 研究采用多种方法：对每位法官分别训练机器学习模型，测量变量重要性指标以确定影响每位法官决策的关键变量，并分析相似案件在不同法官之间的结果一致性。数据来自德克萨斯州哈里斯县的轻罪保释听证会。

Result: 结果显示法官通常表现出算法化行为，其决策可由小型、可解释的公式捕获。然而，在某些案件中法官之间存在显著差异，导致相似被告面临不一致和不平等的待遇。

Conclusion: 识别算法无法解释的司法决策案例，有助于将注意力集中在那些更适合个体化标准而非规则解释结果的决策上，从而改进司法系统的公平性和可预测性。

Abstract: What if judges already behave like algorithms? As artificial intelligence and algorithms are deployed in many settings, including the judicial system, many have debated whether judges should be allowed to rely on them. Instead, we ask whether judges follow predictable, algorithmic-like rules already. If judges already follow consistent, formula-like rules based on discrete and static factors such as criminal history, age, and charge type, then judicial behavior may be improved. However, if judges rely on individualized information that cannot be identified through court data, then standards-based decision-making may be more challenging to understand or improve. This work explores these questions by studying judicial decision-making in misdemeanor bail hearings in Harris County, Texas. Using available court data, we investigate whether magistrate judges follow what resembles an algorithm; whether they consider the same variables in their decision-making; and whether they are consistent with themselves and with each other. To do this, we train machine learning models for each judge, measure variable importance metrics to determine important variables for each judge's decision-making, and analyze outcomes of similar cases for judges. Our results reveal that these judges generally behave algorithmically: their decisions can be captured by small, interpretable formulas. However, in some cases, judges differ substantially, leading to surprising inconsistency and unequal treatment across similar defendants. Identifying cases where algorithms do not explain judicial decision-making can improve the justice system by focusing attention on decisions where individualized standards, rather than rules, better explains outcomes.

</details>


### [232] [TideRL: Boosting Agentic RL Goodput with Readiness-Aware Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10402)
*Yanyu Ren,Xizheng Wang,Xiao Liu,Bowen Lv,Hanchen Zhang,Shudan Zhang,Hanyu Lai,Shuai Wang,Li Chen,Dan Li,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TideRL，一个面向多轮智能体工作负载的弹性强化学习系统，通过连续任务批处理、资源感知的Ref-Actor流水线以及弹性资源缩放三个核心机制，将RL训练的有效吞吐量提升最高5.6倍（相对同步基线）和33%以上（相对异步基线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的强化学习正转向多轮智能体工作负载，这类负载中rollout任务会反复暂停等待外部环境、以不断增长的上下文恢复、并在高度可变的时间完成。在此场景下，RL训练的有效吞吐量（goodput）比原始GPU占用率更重要，因为GPU等待和重复的prefill重计算都是纯开销。

Method: TideRL包含三个核心组件：1) 连续任务批处理（CTB），保留有用的rollout状态；2) 资源感知的Ref-Actor流水线（RA²P），根据就绪队列和到达间隔在解耦流式与聚合放置之间进行选择；3) 弹性资源缩放（ERS），利用相同的就绪信号在rollout和训练之间移动rank。

Result: 在纯文本和多模态智能体工作负载上，TideRL相较同步基线将RL训练有效吞吐量提升最高5.6倍，相较异步基线提升超过33%，同时达到相似的任务性能。此外，KV缓存命中率提升1.58倍，每步训练时间最高减少44.3%，总等待时间最高减少77.6%。

Conclusion: TideRL通过就绪感知的弹性调度和流水线设计，有效解决了多轮智能体RL训练中因环境交互导致的GPU等待和prefill重计算开销问题，显著提升了训练有效吞吐量而无需牺牲任务性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models is moving toward multi-turn agentic workloads, where rollout tasks repeatedly pause for external environments, resume with growing contexts, and finish at highly variable times. In this setting, RL training goodput, measured by training throughput, matters more than raw GPU occupancy: GPU waiting and repeated prefill recomputation are pure overhead. We present TideRL, a readiness-aware elastic RL system with Continuous Task Batching, Resource-Aware Ref-Actor Pipelining, and Elastic Resource Scaling. CTB preserves useful rollout state, $\textrm{RA}^2\textrm{P}$ selects between decoupled streaming and colocated aggregation from the ready backlog and arrival interval, and ERS moves ranks between rollout and training using the same readiness signals. Across text-only and multi-modal agentic workloads, TideRL improves RL training goodput by up to 5.6$\times$ over synchronous baselines and over 33% over asynchronous baselines, while reaching similar task performance. It also improves KV cache hit rate by 1.58$\times$, reduces per-step training time by up to 44.3%, and cuts total waiting time by up to 77.6%.

</details>


### [233] [Actionable Hallucination Detection: Translating Latent Uncertainty into Agentic Critique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10430)
*Sanidhya Vijayvargiya,Rahul Lokesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Critic，一种轻量级LoRA适配器，与冻结的基础LLM并行运行，通过重构transformer残差流来放大潜在接地信号，将模型内部的确定性信号转化为位置化、自然的语言反馈，无需额外推理循环即可实现实时、细粒度的幻觉检测。在工具调用场景下达到0.966 AUROC和>80%的定位准确率，并在ReAct闭环环境中作为低延迟防护栏实现高效的智能体自纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体常出现"用户规范接地失败"问题——执行幻觉性或非期望动作而非表达不确定性，现有检测方法要么无法定位幻觉位置，要么推理延迟过高，无法提供可操作的实时纠错。

Method: 通过轻量级低秩适配器（LoRA）与冻结的基础LLM并行运行，主动重构transformer残差流，放大潜在接地信号并将其转化为同一序列中的局部化自然语言反馈。利用激活修补和逐层探针进行机制分析，将基础模型不确定性几何重构为线性可分离的表示。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama基础模型上验证了检测与下游改进效果，达到0.966 AUROC和>80%定位准确率（如"未接地：日期"），显著优于同等规模的微调外部检测器、语义熵基线和被动内部探针。在ReAct闭环环境中作为低延迟护栏，能在执行前拦截幻觉并进行高效自纠错。

Conclusion: Latent Critic通过秩不变的潜在空间操作将基础模型固有的不确定性信号重构为线性可分离表示，实现优于被动内部探针和外部检测器的可迁移检测能力，为AI智能体提供近零开销的实时幻觉检测与自纠错方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed as AI agents frequently exhibit user specification-grounding failures, executing hallucinated, undesired actions to force a resolution rather than expressing uncertainty. Existing detection methods fail to provide actionable, real-time correction as they either do not localize the hallucinations, or incur prohibitive inference latency. We introduce the Latent Critic, a lightweight low-rank adapter (LoRA) that operates concurrently with a frozen base LLM's generation to actively restructure the transformer's residual stream---amplifying latent grounding signals and translating them into localized, natural language feedback within a single sequence. By refining the base model's native uncertainty signals, this manipulation of the latent space enables reliable, granular detection without the overhead of secondary inference loops. Mechanistic analysis via activation patching and layer-wise probing shows that this rank-invariant behavior restructures pre-existing uncertainty geometry into a linearly separable representation that transfers more reliably than base model representations alone. Using tool-calling as an instantiation of granular hallucinations, we validate the detection and downstream improvements enabled by the Latent Critic architecture across Qwen and Llama-based models. Demonstrating superior real-time efficacy, our approach significantly outperforms equivalent-scale fine-tuned external detectors, semantic entropy baselines, and passive internal probes in isolating hallucinations, achieving 0.966 AUROC and >80% accuracy in localization (e.g., ungrounded: date). When deployed in a closed-loop ReAct environment, the Critic acts as a negligible latency guardrail, intercepting hallucinations before execution to prevent undesired actions while simultaneously leveraging this specific localized feedback to enable efficient agent self-correction.

</details>


### [234] [Do Time-Series Forecasters Use the Right History: Recoverability, Recovery, and Functional Use of Temporal Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10433)
*Qipeng Qian,Yuntao Qian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，即便时间序列模型的预测精度很高且延迟报告正确，也无法保证模型真正使用了正确历史输入。作者提出了输入条件可恢复性度量，并通过理论证明和实验（N-HiTS 55.4%、TCN 92.7%的案例中历史输入在功能上未被使用）揭示预测与真实因果使用之间的脱节，最后提出路由控制方法实现固定的报告对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列模型的预测精度并不能说明模型使用了哪些历史输入来产生预测，即"好预测"与"正确使用历史"之间存在根本性鸿沟。现有研究缺乏对这一问题的系统性分析，作者希望区分三个问题：真实延迟能否从数据中恢复、模型是否报告了它、预测是否实际使用了对应历史。

Method: 首先推导输入条件可恢复性度量，将固有模糊性与模型误差分离；然后通过理论证明展示延迟报告可高度可靠而预测仍使用错误滞后；在有限样本的点延迟任务上进行匹配掩蔽测试，验证历史输入是否被功能性地使用；最后提出通过路由预测经过报告历史、采用硬one-hot控制来实现精确的固定报告对齐。

Result: 理论证明表明，延迟报告可以变得任意可靠、预测风险可接近oracle，但预测器仍使用错误的滞后。在实验中，N-HiTS中55.4%和TCN中92.7%的案例（满足正确延迟报告且归一化超额风险在oracle 10%以内）中，报告的历史在匹配掩蔽测试下功能上未被使用。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：即便预测良好且延迟报告正确，也不能证明模型使用了正确的历史。预测质量与历史使用正确性是两个独立维度，需要专门的机制（如路由控制）才能确保预测与报告历史对齐。

Abstract: Forecast accuracy does not tell us which past inputs produced a prediction. We separate three questions for time-series models with known delay structure: can the true delay be recovered from the observed data, does the model report it, and does the forecast actually use the same history? We first derive input-conditioned recoverability measures that separate intrinsic ambiguity from model error. We then prove that a delay report can become arbitrarily reliable while forecast risk approaches the oracle even though the predictor still uses the wrong lag. This failure also appears in finite samples on the point-delay task: among forecasts with a correct delay report and normalized excess risk within 10\% of the oracle, the reported history is functionally unused under our matched masking test in 55.4\% of N-HiTS cases and 92.7\% of TCN cases. Finally, we show that routing the prediction through the reported history removes off-report bypass paths; a hard one-hot control achieves exact fixed-report alignment. The main conclusion is simple: a good forecast, even with a correct delay report, does not show that the model used the right history.

</details>


### [235] [Detecting an Effect Is Not Learning to Act on It: A Reward-SNR Floor for LLM Acquisition Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10441)
*Ying Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个关键区分：检测一个辅助信号在平均意义上有效，不等于能在每个实例上学会何时使用该信号。当奖励信噪比（reward-SNR）低于检测下限 rho*(N) ≈ 2.8/√N 时，即使信号忠实且存在明显的样本内收益，任何可部署的策略都无法学会按实例获取该信号——学习的路由策略与随机无异，噪声安慰剂可复现至少100%的表观收益。作者引入结构化假设嵌入（SHE）作为实例，在MIND、REES46、Amazon-Beauty三个数据集上验证了该理论，结论是真正可实现的是设计时的机制门控，而非逐实例策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量机器学习管线可以付出逐样本代价获取辅助的模型派生观察（如LLM的结构化推理、慢速oracle、昂贵测量），但需要决定何时使用该信号是有价值的。作者观察到业界常犯的一个错误：将"信号在平均意义上有效"与"能逐实例学习何时获取信号"混为一谈，因此需要厘清二者界限并给出可检测的判定条件。

Method: 论文首先从理论上提出奖励信噪比检测下限 rho*(N) ≈ 2.8/√N，论证路由策略只有在信噪比超过该阈值时才可离线估计。随后引入Structured Hypothesis Embeddings (SHE)框架：冻结的LLM将用户历史转化为排序的、带置信度的、有证据支撑的意图假设，并融合进推荐系统。通过匹配矩的噪声安慰剂对照实验、多种粒度（per-impression、cluster、regime、uplift-tree）的路由学习消融实验，以及在三个公开数据集上的系统性验证来支撑理论主张。

Result: 在per-impression、cluster、regime和uplift-tree四种粒度下，学习到的路由策略均无法超越随机基线；匹配矩的噪声安慰剂至少复现了oracle表观收益的100%，表明表观的"可学习结构"实为噪声的次序统计量。SHE在三个数据集上忠实且可校准，但其价值具有骨干和机制条件性（在ordered GRU上显著+0.0114，95% CI [+0.0030, +0.0209]，但全局冗余差距与零不可区分）。三个数据集均处于信噪比下限之下，导致所有粒度上的学习获取均失败。正对照实验确认这是真实存在的低信噪比极限，而非管线缺陷。

Conclusion: 核心结论是"检测平均效应"与"学习逐实例获取策略"存在根本区别，前者不蕴含后者；奖励信噪比下限 rho*(N) ≈ 2.8/√N 是判断后者是否可行的决定性条件。在这些数据集上真正可实现的设计单元是设计时的机制门控（regime gate），而非逐实例的获取策略。论文发布代码和一键复现命令。

Abstract: Many pipelines can pay a per-example cost to acquire an auxiliary, model-derived observation -- an LLM's structured reasoning, a slow oracle, an expensive measurement -- and then must decide when the acquired signal is worth using. Our thesis is a distinction that is easy to miss: detecting that such a signal helps on average is not the same as learning to act on it per instance, and a reward-SNR floor governs when the second is even possible. Even when the signal is faithful and an in-sample oracle picking the top-b examples by realized reward shows a sizable apparent gain, no deployable policy can learn when to acquire it: across per-impression, cluster, regime, and uplift-tree granularities, learned routing never beats random, and a matched-moment noise placebo reproduces >=100% of the oracle's apparent gain -- the apparent "learnable structure" is order statistics of noise. We explain this with one distinction, detecting a mean effect vs. learning a per-instance acquisition policy, and a reward-SNR detectability floor: routing is estimable offline only if the reward SNR rho clears rho*(N) ~= 2.8/sqrt(N), with a positive control confirming a true low-SNR limit rather than a broken pipeline. As a concrete instantiation we introduce Structured Hypothesis Embeddings (SHE): a frozen LLM turns a user history into ranked, confidence-scored, evidence-grounded intent hypotheses, fused into a recommender. On three public datasets (MIND, REES46, Amazon-Beauty), SHE is faithful and calibratable, yet its value is backbone- and regime-conditional (significant over an ordered GRU, +0.0114, 95% CI [+0.0030, +0.0209], but a global redundancy gap indistinguishable from zero), and learned acquisition collapses at every granularity because all three datasets sit below the floor. The realizable unit is a design-time regime gate, not a per-instance policy. We release code and a one-command reproduction.

</details>


### [236] [A Joint-Distribution Route to Fair Representations with Continuous Sensitive Attributes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10470)
*Yijin Ni,Xiaoming Huo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过联合散度d(P_Z,S, P_Z⊗P_S)来评估连续敏感属性下的公平表示学习，替代现有需要条件分布估计的方法，并以HSIC作为实例实现直接估计，具有O(n^2)闭式解和O(n^{-1/2})收敛速率，相比条件路径方法更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平表示学习方法（如广义人口统计均等、EIPM、互信息等）在处理连续敏感属性时，需要对每个敏感值估计条件分布P_{Z|S=s}与边际P_Z之间的差异，这依赖非参数条件平滑，统计效率低且计算开销大。

Method: 提出用联合散度d(P_{Z,S}, P_Z⊗P_S)替代条件积分泛函来衡量表示Z与敏感属性S的独立性。建立了分解恒等式，证明在可分解的检验函数类上，联合散度等价于EIPM和广义人口统计均等所实例化的条件积分泛函。以HSIC作为联合散度的具体实例，直接从样本估计而无需条件分布估计。

Result: HSIC估计器是闭式O(n^2)统计量，以O(n^{-1/2})速率收敛，优于条件路径估计器的非参数O(n^{-2/5})速率。证明了该实例等价于条件MMD积分并具有显式谱尾。对应的FRHSIC算法在公平-准确率权衡上与条件路径基线相当，但显著减少了每轮训练时间。

Conclusion: 联合散度框架为连续敏感属性的公平表示学习提供了一条不依赖条件分布估计的新路径，在保持公平性效果的同时大幅提升统计效率和计算效率。

Abstract: Fair representation learning with a continuous sensitive attribute $S$ requires a representation $Z$ that is statistically independent of $S$. Existing criteria, including generalized demographic parity, the expectation of integral probability metrics (EIPM), and mutual information, enforce this independence by averaging a per-value discrepancy between the conditional law $P_{Z \mid S=s}$ and the marginal $P_Z$ over the law of $S$. This approach requires a nonparametric surrogate for the conditional law at each sensitive value. We propose evaluating independence through a single joint discrepancy $d\left(P_{Z, S}, P_Z \otimes P_S\right)$ between the joint law and the product of its marginals. We establish a disintegration identity; on decomposable witness classes it equals the conditional-integral functional that EIPM and generalized demographic parity instantiate. By reaching the same target without the conditional law, this discrepancy can be estimated directly from samples via a dependence statistic rather than conditional smoothing. We take the Hilbert-Schmidt independence criterion (HSIC) as an instance of the joint discrepancy $d$ to investigate the statistical efficiency of replacing the conditional formulation. The HSIC estimator is a closed-form $O\left(n^2\right)$ statistic that converges at the $O\left(n^{-1 / 2}\right)$ rate, in contrast to the nonparametric $O\left(n^{-2 / 5}\right)$ rate of the conditional-route estimators. We prove this instance is equivalent to the conditional maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) integral up to an explicit spectral tail. The corresponding algorithmic implementation, i.e., FRHSIC, attains fairness-accuracy tradeoffs comparable to conditional-route basel es while reducing per-epoch training time.

</details>


### [237] [Critic-Free Pretraining for Efficient Online Reinforcement Learning Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10473)
*Daoyi Li,Yixian Zhang,Chao Yu,Wenbo Ding,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Critic-Free Pretraining (CFP)范式，完全摒弃离线Critic训练，让全新初始化的Critic在在线微调中自适应，避免继承离线训练带来的偏差价值估计，从而提升O2O强化学习的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离线到在线(O2O)强化学习中，直接复用离线训练的Critic会产生问题：当策略和数据分布快速变化时，从离线训练继承的价值估计与在线环境不对齐，导致策略改进不准确并降低探索效率。

Method: 提出CFP范式，完全放弃离线Critic训练阶段，使用全新初始化的Critic并允许其在不继承有偏估计的情况下在线适应；该范式与多种主流O2O算法兼容。

Result: CFP在多种任务上与常规O2O算法表现相当或更优，在若干具有挑战性的任务上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 离线Critic训练在O2O微调中并非必要，抛弃Critic预训练可有效避免偏差继承，是一种更高效且广泛兼容的O2O学习范式。

Abstract: Offline-to-online (O2O) reinforcement learning aims to leverage policies pretrained on static datasets while improving them through online interaction. However, directly reusing an offline-trained critic can hinder online fine-tuning: as the policy and data distribution change rapidly, value estimates inherited from offline training may become misaligned with the online environment, leading to inaccurate policy improvement and inefficient exploration. To address this problem, we introduce \textbf{C}ritic-\textbf{F}ree \textbf{P}retraining: an efficient paradigm that completely abandons the approach of offline critic training, allowing a freshly initialized critic to adapt without inheriting biased estimates. CFP is compatible with various mainstream O2O algorithms and consistently matches or improves upon conventional O2O algorithms across a diverse set of tasks, with particularly pronounced gains on several challenging tasks.

</details>


### [238] [Exploration-Driven Personalized Federated Reinforcement Learning via Intrinsic Motivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10499)
*Md Rafid Islam,Rafsan Jany,Zahid Hasan,Ratun Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EDPFRL-IM框架，一种基于内在动机的探索驱动个性化联邦强化学习方法，通过在每个客户端引入基于好奇心的随机网络蒸馏（RND）探索信号来促进本地探索，同时服务器只发送全局探索先验并收集最小化的新颖性摘要，在保护客户端隐私的同时实现多样化和协调的探索，在延迟和稀疏奖励环境中显著优于现有PFRL基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PFRL方法过度依赖已有的强化学习奖励信号来为每个客户端推导最优策略，忽视了非平稳或稀疏奖励环境中的探索问题，导致策略个性化和样本效率受限。

Method: 提出EDPFRL-IM框架：1) 在每个客户端引入基于好奇心驱动的内在探索机制，在原始外在奖励中加入随机网络蒸馏（RND）信号以促进对未探索状态空间的探索；2) 服务器不访问客户端的原始经验或本地梯度估计，仅发送全局探索先验并收集最小化的新颖性摘要，实现客户端间多样化和协调的探索，同时保护隐私。

Result: 在基准环境中的实验表明，该框架在策略个性化和样本效率方面优于平均PFRL基准方法，尤其在延迟奖励和稀疏奖励系统中表现突出。

Conclusion: EDPFRL-IM能够将灵活的探索学习结构整合到联邦强化学习系统中，同时有效保护客户端隐私，为个性化联邦强化学习提供了新的探索驱动范式。

Abstract: Personalized Federated Reinforcement Learning (PFRL) takes a decentralized approach to storing and accessing information based on past experiences while keeping each client's data private during the learning of each client's policy. Many current methods for PFRL rely heavily on exploiting existing reinforcement learning reward signals to derive an optimal policy for each client, thereby neglecting exploration in non-stationary or sparse-reward environments. In this work, we introduce a new exploration-driven framework, Exploration-Driven Personalized Federated Reinforcement Learning via Intrinsic Motivation (EDPFRL-IM), that leverages an inherent curiosity-driven exploration at each client to promote local exploration and protect client privacy. Furthermore, to facilitate policy discovery via exploration in previously unexplored state spaces, clients add an intrinsic random network distillation (RND) signal to their extrinsic reward. Additionally, the server does not have access to clients' raw experiences or local gradient estimates; instead, the server sends global exploration priors and collects minimal novelty summaries from each client to enable both diverse and coordinated exploration among clients. Experiments in benchmark environments show that our framework outperforms average PFRL benchmarks in policy personalization and sample efficiency, primarily in delayed and sparse reward systems. Overall, EDPFRL-IM enables the integration of a flexible exploratory learning structure into federated reinforcement learning systems while preserving client privacy.

</details>


### [239] [Coordinating the Unknown Lipschitz Constant in Multiplayer Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10526)
*Ricardo Parada,Chenzhang Zhao,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究去中心化应用中连续（Lipschitz）动作空间下的合作多智能体bandit问题，假设Lipschitz常数未知。作者考虑了三种信息结构，设计算法估计Lipschitz常数并选择联合动作空间的离散化方案，证明在常见奖励和可观测动作情况下智能体能免费达成一致离散化，而在两者都缺失时可通过抖动量化以不增加主导阶遗憾的代价达成一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化应用（如分布式优化、联邦学习等）中，多个智能体需要在无需通信的情况下合作探索连续动作空间，但Lipschitz常数未知使得离散化成为挑战，核心难题是智能体如何从各自数据中达成相同的离散化选择。

Method: 针对三种信息结构（A.未观测动作+共同奖励；B.可观测动作+独立奖励；C.未观测动作+独立奖励），分别设计算法框架：（1）估计未知的Lipschitz常数；（2）基于估计值选择联合动作空间的离散化方案；（3）将合作bandit方法应用于诱导出的离散问题。对于最困难的结构C，采用抖动量化（dithered quantization）技术保证智能体间离散化的一致性。

Result: 证明了遗憾界（regret guarantees）：在共同奖励或可观测动作的结构下，智能体可以免费（无需额外代价）达成一致的离散化；在两者都缺失的结构C中，通过抖动量化也能以不增加主导阶遗憾为代价实现一致性。

Conclusion: 常见奖励和可观测动作各自都能为智能体提供"免费"的离散化一致性保障；即使两者皆无，仍可通过抖动量化以渐近最优代价实现合作，扩展了合作多智能体bandit在连续动作空间上的理论边界。

Abstract: Motivated by decentralized applications, we study cooperative multi-agent bandits in continuous (Lipschitz) action spaces when the Lipschitz constant is unknown. We consider three information structures: (A)~unobserved actions with common rewards, (B)~observed actions with independent rewards, and (C)~unobserved actions with independent rewards. In each case we design and analyze an algorithm that estimates the Lipschitz constant, chooses a discretization of the joint action space, and applies a cooperative bandit method to the induced discrete problem. Players never communicate once learning starts, so the central difficulty is that they must reach the \emph{same} discretization from their own data. We prove regret guarantees showing that common rewards and observable actions each supply this agreement for free, and that in their absence agreement can still be bought, through a dithered quantization of the estimate, at no cost in the leading order of the regret.

</details>


### [240] [Robust Multi-Agent Bandits with Heavy-Tailed Rewards and Information Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10529)
*Daphne Feng,Ricardo Parada,Lily Jiang,Sophia Yi,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究重尾奖励分布下多智能体多臂老虎机问题，针对三种信息不对称场景（未观测动作共享奖励、观测动作独立奖励、未观测动作独立奖励）设计了鲁棒分散式算法，其遗憾界接近集中式重尾算法的理论最优水平，并通过帕累托分布实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中奖励分布往往具有重尾特征，且多智能体系统中存在信息不对称和去中心化交互，而现有研究大多假设次高斯奖励分布且在集中式框架下进行，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 针对三种信息不对称机制分别设计鲁棒分散式多智能体算法，通过截断/稳健估计等技巧处理重尾奖励，实现接近集中式最优的遗憾界。

Result: 提出的三种分散式算法遗憾界与集中式重尾老虎机的理论下界几乎匹配，且在帕累托分布奖励环境下实验结果验证了理论发现，揭示了同步、协调与探索之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统性地将重尾奖励与多智能体信息不对称场景结合，证明了分散式算法在重尾环境下可以实现接近集中式的性能，为实际应用提供了理论保障。

Abstract: The multi-armed bandit problem is a central framework in sequential decision-making, extensively studied under sub-Gaussian reward assumptions. However, real-world applications often involve heavy-tailed reward distributions and decentralized, information-asymmetric interactions. We study multi-agent multi-armed bandits with heavy-tailed rewards under three information-asymmetry regimes: unobserved actions with common rewards, observed actions with independent rewards, and unobserved actions with independent rewards. We develop robust decentralized algorithms for each setting and derive regret guarantees that nearly match centralized heavy-tailed rates. Experiments on a Pareto-distributed reward environment validate our theoretical findings and illustrate the trade-offs between synchronization, coordination, and exploration across the three regimes.

</details>


### [241] [Retrieval-Corrected Conformal Prediction for Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10553)
*Sangjin Jin,Kangmin Kim,Junhyeong Lee,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出检索修正共形预测（RCCP），一种面向时间序列预测区间的检索增强校准方法。RCCP通过检索相似历史残差作为局部证据，并用标量共形校正来修正覆盖误差，在多个基准上达到目标覆盖率并获得最低的Winkler分数，且计算开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测在时间序列数据上校准效率低下，因为预测误差存在时间依赖且随时间和运行条件变化。现有时间序列共形预测方法虽通过局部校准改进，但广泛的残差加权或额外适应过程可能稀释与当前预测最相关的证据，导致局部校准不够直接。

Method: RCCP包含两个关键步骤：（1）检索阶段：从历史残差中检索相似的一侧残差作为局部证据，构建非对称预测区间；（2）校正阶段：通过标量共形校正对归一化的检索误差进行校准，确定最终所需的覆盖尺度。同时提供了基于归一化检索误差分布稳定性的覆盖间隙界。

Result: 在标准基准和多种骨干预测器上，RCCP在所有设置下均达到目标覆盖率，取得最低的Winkler分数和更少的严重漏报，同时保持低校准和推理开销。
